Xalaat
 
 ut 2026 
 
 
 
Téere yi nekk ci suuf ay cëru xalaat ak gis-gis la yu feeñoon ci sama kaw ci jamono. Duñu yii yu mat seet walla yu am yëngu, waaye mën nañu am ci ëllëg. Walla mën nañu bañ a am, ndax ci yenn xalaat yi, bind ab limite rekk doy na. Yenn yi ay yoon yu tëj lañu te amul solo ñu gën leen a yaatal. Waaye sax mën nañu am solo ngir bind leen.
 
 2023-02-24:  GLITCH YU NEURAL FILTERS
 
Gis naa ko ci lëkkalook liggéey bi, ne bu may gëstu Photoshop ngir gënal ab nataal ci Neural Filters te nataal bi nekk ci CMYK color profile te am ay cër yu leer yu gën a bari, dafay am ab glitch bu yéeme, waaye gën na nekk bu am solo ci wàllu estetig.

 2023-10-04:  GENETIC TRAJECTORIES
 
Dafa ma soppi ay sekans yu genetig nekk ay nataal ci ndëppal bu yomb: A tekki ab tànk ci bëj-gànnaar, T/U ci bëj-saalum, C ci penku ak G ci sowwu. Limu baas yi nag mooy tekki wërsëgu ab yoon bu ànd.

 2024-03-22:  OPTICAL RHYTHM OF COMPUTER WORK
 
Def naa ab porogaraam bu di bind yëngu bu nekk ci klaviye bi ak klik bu nekk ci souris bi ci sama liggéey ci ordinatër bi. Jamono dafay dem ci yoonu bind bi, jóge ci cammoñ dem ci ndeyjoor ak jóge ci kaw dem ci suuf. Ab dëpp bu ñuul tekki na ab duggu, ab barab bu weex tekki na amul.

 2024-08-19:  KUSHÍK'S POETRY
 
Liggéeyoon naa ci ordinatër bi, waaye sama rat Kushík dafa ma doon tàggat saa su nekk. Da doon rëcc ci sama kaw, di laaj xel, di rëcc ci sama taabal, ci sama ordinatër. Dafa ma doon tàmbalee tiis. Kon dafa ma mer, ubbi na ko ab MS Word document bu amul dara te wax ko ne mu bind, bu ko bëggee def ngir man.
 Kushík nag tàmbalee na rëcc ci klaviye bi.
 
Document bii lañu am: koncepty-2024-08-19-kushikova-poezie.pdf [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2024-08-19-kushikova-poezie.pdf]
 
 2024-12-05:  THERMOZEPAM
 
Def naa ngir sama termokamera Seek Thermal RevealPRO ab porogaraam bu boppam ngir seet ak soppi ay nataal yu termografik. Ngir Windows, gisuma woon ab software bu baax ngir kamera bii, loolu nag dafa metti, ndax ay dosiyeem yu termografik dañuy am ay data yu tangay yu ñu mën a liggéey.
 
Tudd naa porogaraam bi Thermozepam, ndax bëgg naa design bi te jëfandikoo ko dafa ma neex lool. Porogaraam bi dafay yeb ay dosiyeem yu termografik TIFF, dafay maye soppi color map bi ci nataal bi jëfandikoo colormap ci library matplotlib, natt tangay bi ci digg bi ci nataal bi, xam tangay bi gën a tuuti ak bi gën a bari, histogram ak bu ñu klikkee, tangay bi ci pixel bu nekk. Ci waxtu yeb bi, dafay mën a génne ab nataal bu PNG bu ñu soppi ay coloram te mën a gën a yaatal nataal bi jëfandikoo interpolace.
 
Screenshot porogaraam bi: koncepty-2024-11-10-thermozepam.png [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/koncepty-2024-11-10-thermozepam.png]
 Source code: koncepty-2024-11-10-thermozepam.pyw [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2024-11-10-thermozepam.pyw]
 
 2025-12-05:  FLASH IN THE DARK
 
Xalaat naa def ab porogaraam Arduino, bu ma defee ci biir ab boyit bu amul dara (bu ñu setaloon ci UV-C lamp) ab laser. Laser boobu dafay génne ab puls bu ab sekond ci jamono bu ñu xamul (ngir man xamuma woon fan la loolu di am, waaye xam ne mën na am ci biir 10 simili ci 100%). Bu ma ko mën a def bu baax, puls bii du ko gis kenn.
 
 2026-01-31:  PINHOLE CAMERA SIMULATION
 
Jéem naa ci Python def ab pinhole camera bu baax. Bi ma nekkee ci lekool bi, defoon naa ab kamera bu dëgg-dëgg, waaye dafa ma meloon ne bu ma ko simulee, dinaa gën a xam ni muy liggéeye, ndax du doy rekk def ab bëtt ci ab boyit, duggal ab kàggu bu am solo, tey def fizik bi liggéeyam (lu mu mën a doon). Fii nag, dafa wara simule fizik bi.
 Dafa ma bëggoon def ab simulasiyoŋ bu gën a nekk dëgg-dëgg, lu dul ne sama xam-xam ak li ma am ci ordinatër bi mooy li ma mën. Porogaraam bi, misaal bi, dafay jëfandikoo forward ray tracing, di topp leer bi jóge ci source bi dem ci kamera bi, lu dul backward ray tracing bi gën a bari, di topp ay ray jóge ci kamera bi dem ci scéne bi. Forward bi dafa gën a nekk ci yoonu fizik bi, waaye dafay metti lool ci wàllu ordinatër.
 
Misaal yu porogaraam bi ak tekki parametre yi: koncepty-2026-01-31-simulace-pinhole-nastaveni.txt [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-01-31-simulace-pinhole-nastaveni.txt]
 Source code: koncepty-2026-01-31-simulace-pinhole.py [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-01-31-simulace-pinhole.py]
 
 2026-03-02:  PIXEL SORTING OF PHOTOS
 
Jéem naa jëfandikoo ab algoritme ngir tànn pixel yi ci nataal yi. Xalaat bi mooy tànn pixel yi ci wàllu HUE ak leer: ci ab yoon (x) tànn pixel yi ci HUE ak ci beneen yoon (y) ci leer. Loolu dafay soppi nataal bi, waaye kenn du ñàkk te kenn du am. Waaye nataal yi dañuy ñàkk seeni tekki, waaye dañuy bàyyi seeni atmosfeer yu color.
 
Source code: koncepty-2026-03-02-trideni-pixelu-fotek.py [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-03-02-trideni-pixelu-fotek.py]
 
 2026-04-13:  RANDOM INTERNET EXPLORATION
 
Lu tollu ci ñaari ayubéer laa doon def ab explorasiyoŋ bu random ci internet bi, ndax dafa ma doon neex xam ni muy mel bu algoritme yi di ko recommandé.
 
Bi njëkk (naïve) mooy def ab porogaraam bu di bind ak ubbi URL, yu ñu génne ci random. Loolu dafa doon lu metti lool, barab bi URL yi mën a nekk dafa gën a bari te yu am yi dañuy bariwul.
 
Bi ñaareel bi mooy def ab crawler bu boppam te jox ko ab seed URL bu yéeme (naka web kdomivolal.cz [https://www.kdomivolal.cz/]). Génne yi nag ma jéem leen a filtré jëfandikoo LLM te seet rekk yu am solo yi (ci ay kriter yu ñu tek). Gis naa ay yéeme yu bari ngir neexal, waaye amul dara lu yéeme.
 
Bi ñatteel bi mooy bi gën a am solo te gën a neex. Gis naa ne am na Certstream. Ab service bu baax la bu di yónnee ay certifikat yu sécurité yu ñu génne ci àdduna bi. Kon dafay wone URL yu web yi, yu yees walla yu yeesal seen certifikat.
 
Source code: koncepty-2026-04-13-certstream-listener.py [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-04-13-certstream-listener.py]
 
Dafa ma jël ay fan yu bari ci di klikk URL yi. Li ma gën a gis mooy ay njaq (kryptoměnové, etc.), porno, AI, AI porno, ay yoon yu ni ñuy defee AI porno, gambling, ay interface yu duggu ci sistem yi, ay web yu tàmbalee (yu am ay placeholder walla ay wax yu mel ne "liggéey nañu ci").
 
Ci yoon bii laa gis web treeyo.my [https://treeyo.my/], bi ma fateliku ndax ni muy mel ak ni muy amul dara ci wàllu attention. Xam naa it ne web boobu dafa nekk ci wetu internet bi, ndax Google indexéwul ko. Internet Archive gis na ko, waaye ci CT log yi, ni man. Seet bi nag am na solo.
 
 2026-05-06:  EXHIBITION IN THE PACIFIC
 
Xalaat naa def 360° panoramatik nataal yu Pacifik yu ñu génne ci AI. Te duggal ci biir ab QR code jëfandikoo AI (lu mel ne Illusion Diffusion), bu di wone ab art. Te mën nañu duggal ci biir ay metadata yu fëgg (naka GPS coordinate ci digg Pacifik) te yeb ci Google Maps.
 
Mën nañu it jël ay barab yu dëgg-dëgg yu ñu nataal ci 360° kamera, naka ci ab dëkk, te soppi seen GPS position ci Pacifik. Li ma di wax ci 360° nataal yi mooy ne Google dafay maye yeb ci GPS position yu dëgg-dëgg jëfandikoo metadata. Waaye nataal yu normal dañu leen wara lëkkalook barab bu am.
 
 2026-06-04:  PIGMENT LESIONS
 
Jéem naa def porogaraam bii, waaye ndax jamono bi, defuma ko. Bind naa rekk sama loxo, tàcc ay pihy (transparent background), bàyyi seeni natt te def ab porogaraam ngir tàmbalee leen ci mgrid. Waaye lii mooy téere bi wara tekki xalaat bi:
 
«Ci atum 2026 laa bind lépp lu ma gis ci sama yaram. Ci object bu nekk, natt bi dañu ko bàyyi. Te lépp lu ñu gis, dañu ko dëppee ci ab grid.»
 
 2026-06-21:  SELF-MODIFYING INTELLIGENT PROGRAM
 
Jéem naa def ab porogaraam bu jëfandikoo OpenAI API ak seeni LLM. Porogaraam bi dafay soppi boppam te tàmbalee ko wat. Te dafa ma doon neex xam fan la loolu di dem.
 Waaye, model bi dafay bañ saa su nekk, ndax dafay xalaat ne dafa am gaar. Walla mu def ko, waaye bi ko topp du ko def. Li ma doon seet mooy rekk bañ bi ndax sécurité. Mënul woon sax dem ba ci ñatteel bi.
 
Source code: koncepty-2026-06-21-sebe-modifikujici-inteligentni-program.py [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-06-21-sebe-modifikujici-inteligentni-program.py]
 
 2026-07-30:  SYSTEM WITH UNIQUE PARTICLES
 
Sistem bi ñu am ci fizik dafa neexul ndax dafa mel ne element yi dañuy mel ni ñoom ñépp. Elektron ni elektron, foton ni foton.
 
Waaye man bëgg naa def ab simulasiyoŋ bu sistem bu am ay element yu bari, waaye amul ñaar yu mel ni ñoom. Ci mujj bi, am naa ab variant bu amul ay cër walla identité. Dañu leen di xam rekk ci seeni interaksiyoŋ, loolu nag ci gëstu bi ci biir sistem bi dafa mel ni loolu.
 
Li loolu di indi dafa ma neex xalaat. Bu ma ko waxee ci yoonu njaq, dëkk ci ab sistem bu mel ni loolu dafa wara metti ngir def ay prediksiyoŋ. Te it, ay entity yu dëkk ci sistem bu mel ni loolu mën nañu wara xool bu baax ngir bañ a ñàkk seeni element.
 
Jéem naa ko simule te def naa ko. Waaye du woon lu am solo. Ci sama xalaat, ndax ngir gis ay yëngu yu am solo, dafa laaj ordinatër bu gën a bari (ay element yu bari, jamono yu bari, mën na nekk ay dimensiyoŋ yu bari) ak gëstu bu gën a xóót.
 
Update jóge ci 2026-08-22: Ci di seet àdduna yi ma génne, gis naa ay structure yu am solo. Dañuy wër walla oval te dañuy mel ni lipid capsule. Dañu am ab biti ak biir, te bariyer bi walla "membrana" bi, bi di def seen biti, dafa dëgër, waaye seen biir dafa gën a yëngu. Seed bii laa def ab valeur bu njëkk ci demo bi ci suuf.
 
Note: yëngu bi ci sistem bi, te loolu mooy rëy bi ci structure yi, dafay lëkkalook ni ñuy wone. Gaawaay bi ci animasiyoŋ bi dafay soppi seen dëgër, mën na nekk ndax ni ñuy natt ci jamono yu bari. Lu dul ne structure yi dañuy rot, misaal bi bu ñu gënee gaawaay bi ci animasiyoŋ bi, sistem bi dafay soppi boppam te def ay yu yees.
 
Lii du lu ma doon xalaat a def. Def naa rekk ab sistem bu am ay částicemi/interakcemi yu amul ni ñoom te lii mooy li génne. Lu baax, am naa ab simulator "lipid".
 
Demo bu nekk ci biir: koncepty-2026-07-30-system-s-unikatnimi-casticemi.html [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-07-30-system-s-unikatnimi-casticemi.html]
 
 2026-08-14:  YI YI YI
 
Loo xalaat bu ma nekkoon di nataal yii yépp yuy sànni ci biir saxaar? Mën nañu leen nataal ak seeni tolluwaay, ba noppi dindi seeni wet, te def leen ci ab mbind bu teew ci seeni tolluwaay.
 
 2026-08-22: 天天中彩票买 
 
Bi ma koy laaj ChatGPT (v5.6 Sol) ci ni ma mënë gëna yombë nettali dogu bu OPTICKÝ RYTMUS PRÁCE NA POČÍTAČI (seetal ci kaw), moom ci boppam la tuddé waxtaan wi «Dogu bu porogaraam  天天中彩票买 ». Te waxtaan wi dafa nekk lu bees loolu ma sos ngir dogu dogu bii dogu. Biir porogaraam bi ma ko yónnee woon dafa nekk lu yoon.
 
Kon ma dogu ne dinaa ko laaj ci loolu te bind lii:
 
«Lu tax nga tuddé waxtaan wii Dogu bu porogaraam  天天中彩票买 ?»
 
Tontu bi mu def:
 
«Xawma. 😭 Teere bi «զ» mooy ab mbindu Armeni bu tudd «za», kon «Dogu bu porogaraam զ» amul benn njariñ ci làkk bu Chek. Turu waxtaan yi dañuy juddu ci boppam ci ndoorteelu waxtaan wi te fii, gëstu bii turu waxtaan bi dafa dogu ne dina dugal ab mbind bu juddu ci Armeni. Ci biir waxtaan wi gisuma ci benn lëkkaloo. Kon déedéet, du ab mbind bu nekk ci biir porogaraam bi. Ab «Dogu bu porogaraam» la rek ak ab mbind bu ñëw ci anam bu ñu xamul.»
 
Bi ma ko laajee yeneen yoon, mu xam ne déedéet, du Armeni, waaye mooy Siin. Waaye mën nañu gis ne ci ndoorteel bi dafa nekkon lu mu xamul. Xalaat naa ne string bi « 天天中彩票买 » dafa mel ni token bu ñu xamul bu tudd « SolidGoldMagikarp ». Waxuma ne dafay liggéey ni moom, te xawma sax ndax tokenizer bi « 天天中彩票买 » dafay nekk ab token bu kenn. Waaye model bi dafay mel ni ChatGPT 3 ak token bi « SolidGoldMagikarp ». Mën na it ne am na lëkkaloo ak xelam, ndax xelam dafay àgg ci bitim waxtaan wii.
 
Waxtaan wii yépp bu ñu ko defee PDF: koncepty-2026-08-22-chatgpt-cely-chat.pdf [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-08-22-chatgpt-cely-chat.pdf]
 
 2026-08-26:  BUTOON BU MA BËGG
 
Damaa nangu ne Windows dafay sonal nit ñi ak ay fenetream yuy laaj ay yëf yu mel ni OneDrive, ay update ak yeneen. Dañuy laaj sa su nekk «Dëgëral» walla «Fatali ma ci ginnaaw». Loolu dafa sonal loolu bu ma bëggul benn ci yii.
 
Kon ma xalaat ne dinaa def ab nataal (fotomontáž) bu ma ci dugal ñatteelu yoon – butoon bu ma bëgg, bu nekkul ci biir sistem bi. Dina nekk butoon bu tudd «Demleen ci seen yoon». Xalaat naa ne duma kesse nit ku lii di neex.
 
 2026-09-18:  KALKULAATËR BU XELAM GËNA SËRIÑ
 
Damaa xalaat yeneen yëf yu amul njariñ yu ma bëgg gëna xool.
 
Ab porogaraam la bu di liggéey ni ab kalkulaatër bu yomb loolu. Mën na nekk lu di boole ay limu yu amul benn yeneen yëf. Waaye lu wuute mooy ne li muy jëlee du lu ñu def ci biir ordinatër bi, waaye dañu koy yónne ab model bu xelam gëna sori (ni ChatGPT-6 Astra). Te moom dina am ndigal ne mu jëlee li mu laaj te dellu ak li mu jëlee.
 
Dafa ma neex loolu, jëfandikoo ab model bu gëna sori ci yëf yu yomb loolu. Ci biir, mën na nekk ab parodi ci ni ñu di joxee yëf yu yomb yépp ci AI te marketing bi di ko tuddé «AI» ni lu am ay kàttan.
 
Ci biir, dina gëna bon ci kalkulaatër bu yoon, dina gëna yomb, dina gëna yéex, dina gëna seer, dina gëna bon ci wàllu gëstu, te dina laaj internet, …

============================================================
Mask yi
 
 Ut 2026 
 
 
 
Nataal yi nekk ci suuf masky [https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Layers_(digital_image_editing)#Layer_mask] yu ñu defar ci Adobe Photoshop bi ma nekk ci liggéey ci ay prooje yu wuute. Mask bu nekk, bi mu juddoo, dañu koo dencoon ni nataal bu wuute. Galeri bii du ay nataal yu ñu tanne. Su fekkee ne mask judd na ci biir liggéey bi, dafa bokk ci. Nataal yi dañu leen tàmbalee ci lu yàgg ba ci lu bees.

============================================================
Ñuuy
 
 me 2026 
 
 
 
At bi muju, bëkk na yoon ci biir bëccëg, damaa daan suuxat ci ab barab ci biir garab yi ci Plzeň-Božkov, ay teey yu ñu toogoon ak ndoxam, ndax bëggumaa tëj yoonu ndox mi.
 
Ci barab boobu, ay golo tàmbli nañu fa dajaloo, di lekk ay xob yu teey yi.
 
Yenn saa yi, danga fay fekk lu tollu ci fukki golo ak ñaar, yenn saa yi itam dara.
 
Dama leen di bokk ak ñoom li ma bëgg.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/prizivnici-obr-1.jpg | golo yi ak teey bi]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/prizivnici-obr-1.jpg]

TITLE: Mbindu Metaweb
SLUG: metawebovy-clanek
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/metawebovy-clanek/
LANGUAGE: wo
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2026-04-22
SOURCE: src/content/posts/metawebovy-clanek.mdx
BUILT_HTML: metawebovy-clanek/index.html
============================================================
Mbindu Metaweb
 
 awril 2026 
 
 
 
MANIFEST
 
Site bi ab jaay la bu ñu jublu ci denc li ne ci biir ngir yagg.
 
Lépp lu ne ci biir dafa nekk ci ay dosiye, te du dox ci ay database walla ay jumtukaay yu jóge bitim réew. Site bii di génn dafa nekk lu ñu man a kopi, denc, ak tabaxaat bu baax.
 
Prose bi dafa tasaaroo ci ay jumtukaay yu nekk ci seen bopp: ay depozitari, ay liggéeyu denc, ak ay snapshot yu nekk ci seen bopp. Loolu dafay wàññi gaar bi ñu man a ñàkk xibaar yi.
 
Jublu bi mooy tax na ñu man a tabaxaat xibaar yi sax bu fekkee ne yenn ci jumtukaay yi ñu ko tabaxee woon dañu fay.
 
DENCU ONLINE
 
Lëkkalekaay yii ñoo ngi teewal benn prose bi ñu denc ci ay jumtukaay yu wuute ak ci ay anam yu wuute.
 
Ñu ngi ciy boole depozitari bu mag, ay kopi ak snapshot yu mat sekk. Ñu ngi sos ab jumtukaay bu am ay yoon yu bari ngir denc prose bi ak tax na ñu man a tabaxaat ko bu fekkee ne yenn ci yoon yi dañu fay.
 
Prose bi dafa am itam ci anam bu Python CLI bu ñu tasaaree ci GitHub ak PyPI, ak ay snapshot yu nekk ci biir ngir denc ay mbind yu ñu tànn.
 
Dencu dijitalu site bii dafay jëfandikoo, ci kaw doméen bi, ay liggéeyu GitHub, GitLab, Codeberg, Neocities, Cloudflare, Netlify, Vercel, Firebase, ChatGPT Sites, Arweave, Pollux.casa (Gemini), envs.net (Gemini + Gopher), Software Heritage, Internet Archive, Webarchiv, Zenodo, Figshare, Permanent.org, Proton.me, YouTube, Spotify, File Garden, Catbox, CYX Share, Ocen Share, Node 616, pOne.Rs, kappa.lol, Lulu, Kobo, PyPI, Perma.cc, archive.today, Ghost Archive, Arquivo.pt, Megalodon / Web Gyotaku ak paste.rs.

DOKUMENTASIOO TEKNIK
 
Dokumentasioo teknik bi ñu soxla ngir tabaxaat site bii.
 
Wone dokumentasioo teknik bu mat sekk [https://vojtamaur.cz/documentation/]
 
PAKETU XIBAR YI NGIR TABAXAAT
 
Paketu xibar yi ngir tabaxaat site bi dafay juddu bu bëgg-bëgg bi amee.
 
Ci biir paketu bi, dafa am kood bu site bi, manifestu asset yi, SHA-256 ak ab skriptu Python bu amul ay soxla yu jóge bitim réew. Du am ay asset yu mag yu jóge public/images/, public/files/ ak public/demos/, ngir baña yokk dayo bu site bi. Bu ñu ko ubbee, skript bi dafay jéem jëkk a kopi asset yi ci biir site bi, bu ko defee mu jëlee leen ci ay mirror yu nekk ci biir internet.
 
Loolu dafay tekki ne kood bi mooy tabax site bi, te site bi mooy tabax kood bi.
 
Paketu xibar yi man nañu ko tabaxaat: bu ñu jëlee asset yi ak soxla yi, man nañu tabaxaat site bi ak yeneen paketu xibar.
 
Jëli paketu xibar yi ngir tabaxaat site bi (vojtamaur-web-source.zip) [https://vojtamaur.cz/source/vojtamaur-web-source.zip]
 
INTEGRITE AK IDENTITE BUILDU BI
 
SHA-256 dafay wone bu fekkee ne dosiye buildu yi dañu soppiku. OpenPGP signature bi dafay may ñu wone ne manifestu kontrol bi dafa am sañ-sañu bindkat bi.
 
SHA256SUMS.txt [https://vojtamaur.cz/SHA256SUMS.txt] — limu SHA-256 kontrolu dosiye buildu yi
 SHA256SUMS.txt.asc [https://vojtamaur.cz/SHA256SUMS.txt.asc] — OpenPGP signature bu wuute ci dosiye SHA256SUMS.txt
 BUILD_SHA256.txt [https://vojtamaur.cz/BUILD_SHA256.txt] — SHA-256 kontrolu dosiye SHA256SUMS.txt
 integrity.json [https://vojtamaur.cz/integrity.json] — xibaar bu mat sekk ci integrite buildu bi
 SIGNING_STATUS.txt [https://vojtamaur.cz/SIGNING_STATUS.txt] — xibaar ci signature bu buildu bi
 vojta-maur-openpgp.asc [https://vojtamaur.cz/keys/vojta-maur-openpgp.asc] — OpenPGP key bu ñu man a wone ngir wone signature bi
 
 Fingerprint bu OpenPGP key bu mag bi:
 57E9 D455 FB10 A228 F66E 18AE C5F5 B390 5220 BE59 
 
Du lépp lu ñu def dafa wara am signature. Buildu bu am signature wara am dosiye SHA256SUMS.txt.asc te signature bi wara am dëgg. Wax ci integrity.json walla SIGNING_STATUS.txt du wone ne signature bi am na dëgg; dosiye yii ay xibaar rekk lañu.
 
EXPORT YI
 
Site bi dafay génne plain-text export bu lépp lu ne ci biir bu nekk bu ñu defee build. Dafa am mbind yi ci anam bu yoon, te du soxla CSS, JavaScript walla yeneen jumtukaay. Dafa nekk dencu bu ndaw ngir index, jang ci offline, ak tabaxaat xibaar yi bu fekkee ne site bi dañu ko sànni. Plain-text export bi dafa baax itam ngir ay modele yu mag yu xibaar.
 
Wone plain-text export bu lépp lu ne ci biir (ALL_POSTS.txt) [https://vojtamaur.cz/ALL_POSTS.txt]
 
Ngir liggéeyu masiin, am na JSON-LD export bu ñu def, bu am mbind yi ci Czech ak Angale, metadata ak lëkkalekaay yi. Dafa jëfandikoo slovnik Schema.org ak yeneen xibaar yu nekk ci slovnik metadata site bi [https://vojtamaur.cz/ns/]. Dafa denc lu nekk ci biir ak lëkkalekaay yi ci diggante làkk yi.
 
Mbind yi ci site bi man nañu leen export ci PDF, bu denc mbind yi ngir man a jang, defar, ak denc bu yagg te du soxla site bi. Xibaar yi ci anam bi ñu ko defee ak anam bi mu am, ñu ngi nekk ci dokumentasioo teknik bi ci kaw.
 
Dafa am itam EPUB export, bu ñu jublu ci ay jumtukaay yu jang ay mbind yu dijital. Dafa am anam bu yoon bu mbind yi, te mu nekk yeneen anam bu mbind yi ci site bi.
 
Dafa am itam SSTV export bu 10. awril 2026, bu defar mbind yi ak nataal yi ci ay xëtt yu nekk ngir SSTV (Slow-Scan Television) ci anam bu PD120 (640 × 496 px). Xëtt yi ñu ngi leen def ci ay WAV, te man nañu leen wone ak SSTV receiver bu amul HTML walla web browser. Export bi mat na 21 GB ak 61 waxtu ci kàddu.
 
Jëli SSTV export (Internet Archive) [https://archive.org/details/vojtamaur-cz-sstv-edition]
 
Ci wetu export yi, am na itam export bu kàddu bu site bi bu 29. ut 2026, bu may jang ak wax ci mbind yi. Zàgg bi dafay denc, ci kaw mbind yi, ay xibaar yu yees, ay xalaat, ak ay xibaar yu nekkul ci site bi.
 
Deglu export bu kàddu bu site bi (YouTube) [https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/xOdeVAuI-Wc]
 
DENCU OFFLINE
 
Yenn ci mbind yi ci site bi dañu nekk ci ay jumtukaay yu nekk ci seen bopp, yu nekk ci bitim internet. Dafa am ay mbind yu ñu def ci kàggu ak ay jumtukaay yu ñu man a yóbbu. Jumtukaay yi dañu wuute ci anam bi ñu leen di jange, soxla yi, ak yagg bi ñu wara yagg. Yenn yi man nañu leen jang bu ñu amul jumtukaay yu dijital, yeneen yi soxla ay jumtukaay yu wuute.
 
Yoon bu nekk ci lim bi dafay wone benn jumtukaay bu nekk ci seen bopp.

Mbaaxu clipboard
 
 awril 2026 
 
 
 
Prose bi dafa koy liggéey ak li nekk ci biir clipboard bu Windows bi  [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/Schr%C3%A1nka_(software)].
 Bépp téere, nataal walla dëppoo bu ñu ko kopiye, dañu koy dawal dugal ci ab barab bu ñu yamale.
 Li nekk ci biir duñu ko soppi walla di ko séddale. Yenn yi ci yenn yi dañuy duggu ci biir form bi te di nekk ci kaw yeneen yi.
 Plat bi dafay fees lëpp te amul yoon bu ñu koy dellu ginaaw walla di ko soppi.
 
Bépp nataal bu nekk ci suuf, ab njariñ la bu bés bu nekk, te di jël li ma liggéey ci sama ordinatër.

Code bu ñu jóge
 Code Python bu ñu jóge ngir prose bi nekk na fi:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import os
import random
import textwrap
from datetime import datetime
import tkinter as tk

import pyperclip
from PIL import Image, ImageTk, ImageGrab, ImageDraw, ImageFont

# =========================
# Nastavení
# =========================
CANVAS_SIZE = 700
BG_COLOR = "white"
POLL_MS = 500
MAX_IMAGE_SIZE = 320
TEXT_WRAP_CHARS = 38
TEXT_FONT = ("Arial", 14)
TEXT_COLOR = "black"
FILE_COLOR = "#1f4e79"

# =========================
# Tkinter okno
# =========================
root = tk.Tk()
root.title("Clipboard Canvas")
root.geometry(f"{CANVAS_SIZE}x{CANVAS_SIZE}")

canvas = tk.Canvas(root, width=CANVAS_SIZE, height=CANVAS_SIZE, bg=BG_COLOR, highlightthickness=0)
canvas.pack(fill="both", expand=True)

# =========================
# PIL "skutečné" plátno pro export do PNG
# =========================
board = Image.new("RGB", (CANVAS_SIZE, CANVAS_SIZE), "white")
board_draw = ImageDraw.Draw(board)

try:
    pil_font = ImageFont.truetype("arial.ttf", 20)
except Exception:
    pil_font = ImageFont.load_default()

# Tohle je důležité: držíme reference na VŠECHNY Tk obrázky,
# jinak Tkinter staré obrázky "uklidí" a zmizí.
tk_images = []

last_text = None
last_image_hash = None
last_file_list = None

# =========================
# Pomocné funkce
# =========================
def get_random_position(item_w, item_h):
    """Vrátí náhodnou pozici tak, aby se objekt vešel celý do plátna."""
    item_w = min(item_w, CANVAS_SIZE)
    item_h = min(item_h, CANVAS_SIZE)

    max_x = max(0, CANVAS_SIZE - item_w)
    max_y = max(0, CANVAS_SIZE - item_h)

    x = random.randint(0, max_x)
    y = random.randint(0, max_y)
    return x, y

def wrap_text_for_display(text, width=TEXT_WRAP_CHARS):
    lines = []
    for paragraph in text.splitlines() or [""]:
        wrapped = textwrap.wrap(paragraph, width=width) or [""]
        lines.extend(wrapped)
    return "\n".join(lines)

def measure_text_block(text):
    """Hrubý odhad velikosti textového bloku pro umístění."""
    lines = text.split("\n")
    max_len = max((len(line) for line in lines), default=1)
    w = min(CANVAS_SIZE, 12 + max_len * 9)
    h = 12 + len(lines) * 24
    return w, h

def draw_text_on_canvas_and_board(text, color=TEXT_COLOR, prefix=None):
    display_text = f"{prefix}{text}" if prefix else text
    wrapped = wrap_text_for_display(display_text)
    w, h = measure_text_block(wrapped)
    x, y = get_random_position(w, h)

    # Tkinter canvas
    canvas.create_text(
        x, y,
        anchor="nw",
        text=wrapped,
        fill=color,
        font=TEXT_FONT,
        width=w
    )

    # PIL board
    board_draw.multiline_text((x, y), wrapped, fill=color, font=pil_font, spacing=6)

def draw_image_on_canvas_and_board(img):
    # zmenšíme kopii, originál neni potřeba
    img = img.convert("RGB")
    img.thumbnail((MAX_IMAGE_SIZE, MAX_IMAGE_SIZE))

    w, h = img.size
    x, y = get_random_position(w, h)

    # Tkinter canvas
    tk_img = ImageTk.PhotoImage(img)
    tk_images.append(tk_img)
    canvas.create_image(x, y, anchor="nw", image=tk_img)

    # PIL board
    board.paste(img, (x, y))

def hash_pil_image(img):
    # Rychlejší než hash(img.tobytes()) na obrovských datech:
    tiny = img.copy()
    tiny.thumbnail((64, 64))
    return hash(tiny.tobytes())

def save_canvas_png(event=None):
    filename = f"clipboard_canvas_{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S')}.png"
    board.save(filename, "PNG")
    print(f"Uloženo: {os.path.abspath(filename)}")
    
# =========================
# Clipboard monitoring
# =========================
def check_clipboard():
    global last_text, last_image_hash, last_file_list

    # 1) Zkusit obrázek / seznam souborů
    try:
        clip = ImageGrab.grabclipboard()

        # Obrázek
        if isinstance(clip, Image.Image):
            img_hash = hash_pil_image(clip)
            if img_hash != last_image_hash:
                last_image_hash = img_hash
                draw_image_on_canvas_and_board(clip)

        # Soubory
        elif isinstance(clip, list):
            normalized = tuple(map(str, clip))
            if normalized != last_file_list:
                last_file_list = normalized
                for path in normalized:
                    draw_text_on_canvas_and_board(path, color=FILE_COLOR, prefix="[FILE] ")
    except Exception:
        pass

    # 2) Zkusit text
    try:
        text = pyperclip.paste()
        if isinstance(text, str):
            text = text.strip()
            if text and text != last_text:
                last_text = text
                draw_text_on_canvas_and_board(text)
    except Exception:
        pass

    root.after(POLL_MS, check_clipboard)

# =========================
# Klávesy
# =========================
root.bind("<KeyPress-s>", save_canvas_png)
root.bind("<KeyPress-S>", save_canvas_png)
root.focus_force()

# start
check_clipboard()
root.mainloop()
[/CODE BLOCK]

TITLE: Yoon yi di dund
SLUG: docasne-entity
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/docasne-entity/
LANGUAGE: wo
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2026-01-02
SOURCE: src/content/posts/docasne-entity.mdx
BUILT_HTML: docasne-entity/index.html
============================================================
Yoon yi di dund
 
 samwiye 2026 
 
 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/TS4kEbk3SGs]
 
 
Prose bi mooy wone ni Boltzmann bop yi  [https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Boltzmann_brain] ak yeneen yoon yi di dund di juddoo, yuy feeñ ci biir entropie ci anam bu amul ndigal, ba noppi dëppoo ci biir.
 
Ci biir àdduna bi ñu nekk, gis loolu day laaj jamono ju yàgg lool.
 
Loolu tax ma soppi gaawaay bu partikil yépp, ngir mën a wone juddu ak dee gu téemeeri yoon yi ci jamono ju mat. Dëkkëndoo bi ma def ci yooni partikil yi amul.
 
Yoon yi juddoo nañu ci ni ma tànnee téemeeri baat yu amul ndigal ci diksioner, jox lépp ab nataal bu koy wone, ba noppi may leen ñu judduwaat ci biir barab bu entropie bi.
 
Ab jéego la ngir wone ni li gën a rëy, melokaan walla xalaat mën na feeñ ci biir yéwéne, te amul benn yéene ci nekk gu sax.

TITLE: Genom ci spiralu Theodor
SLUG: genom-theodorove-spirale

SECTION: volna-tvorba

============================================================
Genom ci spiralu Theodor
 
 samwiyee 2026 
 
 
 
Visualisation bii dafay soppi xibaaru genetique bu lineaire (DNA/RNA) ba mu nekku ci biir 2D spiralu Theodor  [https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Spiral_of_Theodorus].
 
Biiro bu nekk ci spiral bi dafay tekki benn base ci anam bi mu nekke ci biir sekans bi. Tambaliinu sekans bi ngi ci biir spiral bi. Coloru biiro bi dafay wonn xeetu base bi.
 
Spiral bii dafay maye wonn 1D xibaar yu gudd ci 2D surface bu tuuti, te duñu tàccu ci biir xibaar bi. Amul ay tàccu yu ñu def, ni melni dellu ci ndoorteelu layn bi, yu gëna bari ci anam yu ñu bindee text.
 
Ni misaal, ma ngi wonn genomu ñetti organism yu am gudd yu wuute. Ñepp ñu am genom bu lineaire, bii mooy anam bi gëna baax ngir visualisation bii.
 
HIV-1: [https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/nuccore/170878295]

Code source
 Code source Python bi ngir projet bii ngi fi:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from collections import Counter
from pathlib import Path

# =========================
# KONFIG
# =========================

SEQ_TXT_PATH = r"ebola_virus_complete_genome.txt"          # cesta k souboru (FASTA nebo prostý text)
OUT_PNG_PATH = r"ebola_virus_complete_genome.png"          # kam uložit PNG výstup

WIDTH_PX  = 4000
HEIGHT_PX = 4000

SHOW_LEGEND = False  # defaultně žádný text
LEGEND_TEXT = "genom na Theodorově spirále"

# RGBK (A,C,G,T/U -> R,G,B,K)
COLORS = {
    "A": (1.0, 0.0, 0.0),  # Red
    "C": (0.0, 1.0, 0.0),  # Green
    "G": (0.0, 0.0, 1.0),  # Blue
    "T": (0.0, 0.0, 0.0),  # Black (Key)
    "U": (0.0, 0.0, 0.0),  # RNA U taky do K
}

# Vzhled
DRAW_SPIRAL_LINE = False
LINE_WIDTH = 0.12
LINE_ALPHA = 0.25

POINT_SIZE = 90.0
POINT_ALPHA = 1

CROP_PADDING_RATIO = 0.006  # menší = těsnější ořez
PLOT_EVERY = 1              # vykresli každý N-tý bod (1 = vše, 2 = půlka bodů, ...)

BACKGROUND = "white"        # nebo "black"
TRANSPARENT_BG = False      # true = průhledné PNG

# =========================
# IMPLEMENTACE
# =========================

def read_sequence(path: str) -> str:
    """Načte sekvenci a nechá jen A/C/G/T/U."""
    txt = Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore").upper()
    seq = "".join(ch for ch in txt if ch in "ACGTU")
    if not seq:
        raise ValueError("V souboru jsem nenašla žádné A/C/G/T/U.")
    return seq

def theodorus_vertices(n: int) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
    """Souřadnice vrcholů Theodorovy spirály pro indexy 0..n (0 = origin)."""
    theta = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=float)
    if n >= 2:
        theta[2:] = np.cumsum(np.arctan(1.0 / np.sqrt(np.arange(1, n))))
    r = np.sqrt(np.arange(0, n + 1, dtype=float))
    x = r * np.cos(theta)
    y = r * np.sin(theta)
    return x, y

def main():
    seq = read_sequence(SEQ_TXT_PATH)
    if PLOT_EVERY > 1:
        seq = seq[::PLOT_EVERY]

    n = len(seq)
    x, y = theodorus_vertices(n)

    pos = np.arange(1, n + 1)  # vrchol i mapuje seq[i-1]
    seq_bytes = np.frombuffer(seq.encode("ascii"), dtype=np.uint8)
    counts = Counter(seq)

    # Figsize = pixely / dpi; dpi si zvolíme jen jako interní převod.
    dpi_internal = 100
    figsize = (WIDTH_PX / dpi_internal, HEIGHT_PX / dpi_internal)

    fig = plt.figure(figsize=figsize, dpi=dpi_internal, facecolor=BACKGROUND)
    ax = fig.add_axes([0, 0, 1, 1])  # žádné okraje, čistý full-bleed
    ax.set_facecolor(BACKGROUND)

    if DRAW_SPIRAL_LINE:
        ax.plot(
            x[1:], y[1:],
            linewidth=LINE_WIDTH,
            color=(0, 0, 0),
            alpha=LINE_ALPHA
        )

    for base in ["A", "C", "G", "T", "U"]:
        mask = (seq_bytes == ord(base))
        if not np.any(mask):
            continue
        idxs = pos[mask]
        ax.scatter(
            x[idxs], y[idxs],
            s=POINT_SIZE,
            c=[COLORS[base]],
            alpha=POINT_ALPHA,
            linewidths=0
        )

    # Tight crop + vycentrování na formát
    xmin, xmax = x[1:].min(), x[1:].max()
    ymin, ymax = y[1:].min(), y[1:].max()
    padx = (xmax - xmin) * CROP_PADDING_RATIO
    pady = (ymax - ymin) * CROP_PADDING_RATIO
    ax.set_xlim(xmin - padx, xmax + padx)
    ax.set_ylim(ymin - pady, ymax + pady)

    ax.set_aspect("equal", adjustable="box")
    ax.axis("off")

    if SHOW_LEGEND:
        title_counts = ", ".join(f"{b}:{counts.get(b, 0)}" for b in ["A", "C", "G", "T", "U"])
        ax.text(
            0.5, 0.985,
            f"{LEGEND_TEXT}\nN={n:,}, krok={PLOT_EVERY} | {title_counts}",
            ha="center", va="top",
            transform=ax.transAxes,
            fontsize=14,
            color="black" if BACKGROUND == "white" else "white"
        )

    fig.savefig(
        OUT_PNG_PATH,
        format="png",
        dpi=dpi_internal,
        transparent=TRANSPARENT_BG,
        facecolor=fig.get_facecolor()
    )
    plt.close(fig)
    print(f"Uloženo: {OUT_PNG_PATH}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

[/CODE BLOCK]

TITLE: Anti-lakk
SLUG: anti-jazyk
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/anti-jazyk/
LANGUAGE: wo
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2025-12-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/anti-jazyk.mdx
BUILT_HTML: anti-jazyk/index.html
============================================================
Anti-lakk
 
 desàmbar 2025 
 
 
 
Modèl yu lakk yu ñu gën a xam dañuy tànn token bu topp ci anam bu gën a nekk, di tànn benn ci li gën a am solo ci li ñu xalaat ne mooy gën a ànd ak li ñu koy wax. Loolu lañuy woowee standard sampling.
 Prose bii mooy def li gën a wute ak loolu.
 Ci wàllu tànn token yu gën a am solo, dañuy sonal modèl bi ba mu tànn yu gën a néew solo, di yu modèl bi du mës a jëfandikoo ci anam bu normal.
 Li ci juddoo mooy anti-jazyk – téere bu juddoo ci wàll yu gën a sori, yu ñu tàggat, te di yu bari ci wàllu lakk bi modèl bi am.
 Loolu di jur ay anam yu nekkul ay symbol yu ñu tànn ci anam bu neex, waaye du it ay wax yu am solo.
 
Naka la koy defee
 Modèl bi day jot ab duggu bu gàtt (p.l. „This is “).
 Day génne ab wàllu li gën a am solo ci token yu mën a topp.
 Ci wàllu tànn ci kaw (token yu gën a am solo), mbooleem anam bi day soppi te di tànn ci suuf (token yu gën a néew solo).
 Loolu day feeñal „wàll yu suuf“ yi ci modèl bi: ay wax yu néew a am, ay wàllu lakk ak yeneen yëf yi modèl bi di tëj ci anam bu normal.
 
Kood
 Mën nga doxal kood bi ci Google Colab:
 https://colab.research.google.com/drive/18y4hUsNv-aEZHtXVp67ygHGGpMBkByxD?usp=sharing
 
Colab fi day liggéeyal rekk ngir doxal ko. Du mooy kood bi ñu denc ci anam bu wér.
 Loolu tax lañu denc itam anam bi ci suuf ci biir téere bi.
 
Kood bi ñu séddale ko ci ñetti wàll yu wuute ni mu nekkee ci notebook bi. Bi njëkk bi day yeb modèl bi, bi ñaareel bi day génne téere, bi ñatteel bi day denc li mujj a génn.
 
1. Yeb modèl bi ak tokenizer bi
 Wàll bii mën nga ko doxal yoonu benn. Day yeb modèl GPT-2 ak tokenizer bi.
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import torch
from transformers import AutoTokenizer, AutoModelForCausalLM

tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained("gpt2")  # also works: gpt2-medium, gpt2-large, gpt2-xl, EleutherAI/gpt-neo-1.3B
model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained("gpt2")
model.eval()
[/CODE BLOCK]
 
2. Génne anti-lakk
 Ci wàll bii mën nga soppi téere bi ñu duggal, limu token yu bees yi ak value bu bottom_k.
 Bu bottom_k gënee néew, la modèl bi gëna sonn ba dem ba ci suuf lool ci wàllu li gën a am solo.
 
[CODE BLOCK]
prompt = "This is "
input_ids = tokenizer.encode(prompt, return_tensors="pt")

max_new_tokens = 50
bottom_k = 5

with torch.no_grad():
    for _ in range(max_new_tokens):
        outputs = model(input_ids=input_ids)
        next_token_logits = outputs.logits[0, -1, :]

        probs = torch.softmax(next_token_logits, dim=-1)

        bottom_probs, bottom_indices = torch.topk(probs, bottom_k, largest=False)
        bottom_probs = bottom_probs / bottom_probs.sum()

        chosen_index = torch.multinomial(bottom_probs, num_samples=1)
        next_token_id = bottom_indices[chosen_index]

        input_ids = torch.cat([input_ids, next_token_id.unsqueeze(0)], dim=-1)

generated_text = tokenizer.decode(input_ids.squeeze(), skip_special_tokens=True)
print(generated_text)
[/CODE BLOCK]
 
3. Denc li mujj a génn
 Wàll bii day denc téere bi mujj a génn ci file bu output.txt.
 
[CODE BLOCK]
with open("output.txt", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    f.write(generated_text)
[/CODE BLOCK]
 
Wàllu GPT-2 bokkul ci site bi. Bu ñu koy doxal, day génn ci yeneen barab.
 Fi lañu denc anam bi: ab njuumte bu gàtt, naka lañuy sonale modèl bi ba mu génne ay lakk yu amul solo.
 
Li génne
 Fi jëfandikoo naa modèl GPT-2 te may ko mu yokk téere bi „This is “:

Analys bu li génne
 Anti-lakk du ay wax yu ñu tànn ci anam bu neex. Ay wàll yu bari dañuy mel ni ay yoon yu am ci ay data yu wuute yi modèl bi mën na gis, waaye sampling bu normal du ko mës a woo ndax dañuy néew solo. Anti-lakk bi nak mooy gën a am solo.
 Yenn wàll yu ci téere bi modèl bi di génne dañuy mel ni ay yëf yu jóge ci dataset bi ñu ko tàggatee. Yenn saa yi mën nga gis ay yoon yu jóge ci ay e-shop („BuyableInstoreAndOnline“, „SpecialOrderable“, „ItemThumbnailImage“), ay yëf yu mën a am solo walla yu am solo ci wàllu askan („sexual“, „Muslims“, „Hispanic“), ay term yu science walla ay wàllam („isomer“, „antidepress“, „[m]icrobial“, „[p]uncture“, „intolerance“), ay yëf yu culture („Monroe“, „Phelps“, „Rockefeller“, „Nitrome“), ay yëf yu diine walla ay yëf yu fantasy („Archdemon“, „devils“), ay barab („Moroc“, „Afric“), ay wàll yu interface yu site yu web („cloneembedreportprint“, „IMAGES“) ak yeneen.
 Dafa am solo ñu wax ne analys bii am na ay dogu: xel bi dafa am yoonu tànn ay yëf yu ñu xam (ay brand, ay tur, ay xalaat) te bàyyi li des. Yenn wax yi itam dañuy am ay tekki yu wuute („intolerance“ mën na nekk ci wàllu wér-gu-yaram walla ci wàllu askan), loolu tax tekki bi mënul nekk bu wér.

Worm ε
 
 sëttambur 2025 
 
 
 
Definisiyo:
 Li ko tax aju mooy open-source neuron network bu worm bi tudd C. elegans. Waay bu nekk wᵢ′ dañu koy soppi:
 wᵢ′ = wᵢ + εᵢ
 fi nu ne εᵢ mooy variable bu amul ndigal bu am distribution bu yam ci diggante [−10⁻⁴,10⁻⁴].
 
Motivasiyo:
 Doli ab lim bu tuuti te duñu mën a lim εᵢ du ngir gënël model bi. Li ko tax aju rekk mooy am worm bu nu mënul a lim ba pare. Loolu rekk mooy njariñam.

TITLE: DullGPT
SLUG: dullgpt
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/dullgpt/
LANGUAGE: wo
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2024-07-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/dullgpt.mdx
BUILT_HTML: dullgpt/index.html
============================================================
DullGPT
 
 suwe 2024 
 
 
 
DullGPT mo ab xalaat bu ñu defar ngir wone ne man nañu sos ab njuumtu bu xam-xam (AI) ci anam bu yomb lool (te bu amul solo lool). Proje bi dafa am ab porogaraam bu di sos lépp lu man a doon, di ko dugal ci ChatGPT te di denc tontu yi mu jële. Ci anam yii la di tabax ab banku xibaar bu am ay laaj ak tontu, di sos ab njuumtu bu mel ni ku am xel.
 
DullGPT am na ñaari anam: "jang" ak "jëfandikoo". Ci anam bu jang, porogaraam bi dafay sos ay laaj te di dajale tontu yi, te ci anam bu jëfandikoo, dafay may nit ki mu dugal ay laaj te jot tontu yi ci banku xibaar bi.
 
Lii ab njuumtu la bu ñu jëfandikoo ngir wone ne man nañu topp ci anam bu gëna gatt ngir defar xel. Teewul mu di jéem a xam li laaj bi tekki, dafay dajale rek ay laaj ak tontu yu bari lool. Ci anam bu ñu koy xool, war na mel ni ChatGPT bu amoon ay xibaar yu bari. Lii dafay laaj ndax xam-xam ak xel dëgg-dëgg am nañu solo ngir am ab waxtaan bu am xel. Ci leneen, DullGPT dafay joxe tontu yu mel ni yu ab njuumtu bu am xel, waaye amul benn xel walla xalaat bu dëgg-dëgg.
 
DullGPT, ci wàllu ChatGPT, am na ay dog-dog yu bari, rawatina ci wàllu "jang" ak ni mu soxla yeneen njuumtu yu mag, te du xool li ñu waxtaané woon, te du xool lépp lu ñu dugal (ni ay bindu sinoo). Te itam, du soppi tontu bi ci laaj bi ñu ko joxe. Ndax lii ab xalaat rek la te du lu ñu man a jëfandikoo ci dëgg-dëgg, amul solo ñu jéem a faj dog-dog yii.
 
Dafa am solo a wax ne, bëgguma a jëfandikoo lii ngir dëgërël xalaatu "Chinese Room", bi di laaj ndax man nañu am xel dëgg-dëgg ci anam bu di manipulee ay bindu rek te xamuñu li ñu tekki. Dama di wax rek ne, man nañu sos ab njuumtu bu am xel, sax bu fekke anam bi amul solo te dof na. Waaye xalaatuma ne lépp lu ñu sos war na di amul dara ci biir.
 
Lii mooy kood bi sos porogaraam bi:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import openai
import sqlite3
import string
import itertools
import sys
import os
import pickle

# Nastavení API klíče
OPENAI_API_KEY = "SEM VLOŽ SVŮJ OPENAI API KEY"
openai.api_key = OPENAI_API_KEY

# Cesta k databázi a log souboru
DATABASE_PATH = "dullgpt_db.sqlite"
LOG_FILE = "log.txt"
LAST_INPUT_FILE = "last_input.pkl"

# Funkce pro komunikaci s OpenAI API
def query_chatgpt(prompt):
    response = openai.ChatCompletion.create(
        model="gpt-3.5-turbo",
        messages=[
            {"role": "system", "content": "You are a helpful assistant."},
            {"role": "user", "content": prompt}
        ]
    )
    return response['choices'][0]['message']['content'].strip()

# Funkce pro generování vstupů
def generate_inputs(length):
    characters = string.ascii_letters + string.digits + string.punctuation + " "
    return [''.join(i) for i in itertools.product(characters, repeat=length)]

# Funkce pro vytvoření databáze
def create_db():
    conn = sqlite3.connect(DATABASE_PATH)
    c = conn.cursor()
    c.execute('''
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS responses (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
            input TEXT NOT NULL,
            output TEXT NOT NULL
        )
    ''')
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()

# Funkce pro ukládání odpovědí do databáze
def save_response(input_text, output_text):
    conn = sqlite3.connect(DATABASE_PATH)
    c = conn.cursor()
    c.execute('''
        INSERT INTO responses (input, output) VALUES (?, ?)
    ''', (input_text, output_text))
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()

# Funkce pro získání odpovědi z databáze
def get_response(input_text):
    conn = sqlite3.connect(DATABASE_PATH)
    c = conn.cursor()
    c.execute('''
        SELECT output FROM responses WHERE input = ?
    ''', (input_text,))
    result = c.fetchone()
    conn.close()
    return result[0] if result else None

# Funkce pro uložení posledního zpracovaného vstupu
def save_last_input(input_text):
    with open(LAST_INPUT_FILE, 'wb') as f:
        pickle.dump(input_text, f)

# Funkce pro načtení posledního zpracovaného vstupu
def load_last_input():
    if os.path.exists(LAST_INPUT_FILE):
        with open(LAST_INPUT_FILE, 'rb') as f:
            return pickle.load(f)
    return None

# Funkce pro logování párů vstup-výstup
def log_response(input_text, output_text):
    with open(LOG_FILE, 'a', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        f.write(f"Input: {input_text}, Output: {output_text}\n")

# Režim "učení"
def learn_mode():
    create_db()
    last_input = load_last_input()
    start_length = 1 if not last_input else len(last_input)
    start_index = 0

    if last_input:
        print(f"Loaded last input: '{last_input}'")
        all_inputs = generate_inputs(len(last_input))
        if last_input in all_inputs:
            start_index = all_inputs.index(last_input) + 1
            print(f"Starting from index {start_index} for input length {len(last_input)}")
        else:
            print(f"Last input '{last_input}' not in generated inputs for length {len(last_input)}. Starting from beginning.")
            start_length = len(last_input) + 1
            last_input = None  # Reset last_input to start from the beginning

    while True:  # Loop to continue processing inputs of increasing length
        inputs = generate_inputs(start_length)
        if last_input and start_length == len(last_input):
            start_index = inputs.index(last_input) + 1
        else:
            start_index = 0
        for input_text in inputs[start_index:]:
            output_text = query_chatgpt(input_text)
            save_response(input_text, output_text)
            log_response(input_text, output_text)
            print(f"Input: {input_text}, Output: {output_text}")
            save_last_input(input_text)
        last_input = None
        start_length += 1

# Režim "užívání"
def use_mode():
    create_db()
    while True:
        user_input = input("Enter your input: ")
        output_text = get_response(user_input)
        if output_text:
            print(f"Output: {output_text}")
        else:
            print("No response found for this input.")

# Hlavní část programu
if __name__ == "__main__":
    if len(sys.argv) != 2 or sys.argv[1] not in ("learn", "use"):
        print("Usage: python dullgpt.py [learn|use]")
        sys.exit(1)
    
    if sys.argv[1] == "learn":
        learn_mode()
    else:
        use_mode()
[/CODE BLOCK]
 
 
Te lii mooy lu njëkk ci 1000 laaj ak tontu yi nekk ci log bi diiru log bu amul jeexul bi amul jeexul bii log bi amul jeex:
  
[CODE BLOCK]
Input: a, Output: How can I assist you today?
Input: b, Output: How can I assist you further?
Input: c, Output: Hello! How can I assist you today?
Input: d, Output: Hello! How can I assist you today?
Input: e, Output: Hello! How can I assist you today?
Input: f, Output: Hello! How can I assist you today?
Input: g, Output: Hello! How can I assist you today?
Input: h, Output: Hello! How can I assist you today?
Input: i, Output: Hello! How can I assist you today?

Sëqëru kërë
 
 féewari 2024 
 
 
 
Def naa ab porogaraam bu yomb boobu mën nga ko jox benn walla ay kër-gëstu. Dina seeti lépp ci biir kër-gëstu yii, ba ci biir kër-gëstu yu néew yi, te bu fekkee am na nataal (nataal fi damay tekki ab dëppoo bu am ab tàmbali bu mel ni .jpg, .jpeg, .png, .tif, .tiff), dina yokk yoonam ci biir mbooleem nataal yi. Bu mu paree ci seeti gi, dina tànn benn nataal rek ci biir yii te won ko.
 
Gannaaw loolu, jokkal naa ci sama ordinatëer mbooleem harddisk yi amoon ay kàttan yu nataal yi ma dëgëraloon ci sama dund (léegi am nañu lu ëpp 91 000 ay nataal). Lépp lu harddisk yi amoon, def naa ko mu nekk kër-gëstu yu tàmbali yi porogaraam bi di tànn nataal yi. Ngir ay mbir yu praktig, waroon naa tere tànn ci yenn kër-gëstu yi, ndax yenn yi ay data yu porogaraam rek lañu amoon, ni ay ikoonam. Bu ma toggee ci klawiyee bi ENTER, porogaraam bi dina tànn ab nataal bu amul yoon ci sama dund. Bu ma toggee wat ENTER, porogaraam bi dina tànn beneen nataal, te yeneen...
 
Ci nataal yi ma tànn ci yoon, def naa ay seri yu bari yuy ànd ak yeneen. Seri bu nekk dina tàmbali ak tànn bu amul yoon ci nataal te di gëstu ci tànn yeneen nataal, ba bu fekkee am na luy dëppoo ak luy amul yoon, loolu ma bëggul won ci biir mbooloo. Nataal bi mujj ci seri bi, dañu koy won ci wàllu gëstu bi mu mel ni lu ñuul. Dama doon laaj, lan mooy nataal yi di tax seri yi jeex te lu tax. Ci lu ëpp, ci sama yéeme, loolu dafa jeex ndax ragal bi ma ragaloon ci sëqëru kër-gëstu yeneen nit ñi. Ci yeneen yoon, loolu dafa jeex ndax nataal yi amoon ay dokimaa yu liggéey, kër-gëstu yeneen nit ñi ak sama kër, te yeneen...
 
Ci porogaraam bi, xam naa ni sëqëru sama kër-gëstu dafa mel ni lu lëndëm, bu fekkee am na sax. Ci benn waxtu, waroon naa tànn ndax dinaa won nataal bi walla déedéet. Tànn gii nag dafa doonoon lu jafe te lu amul yoon.

Kood bu tàmbali
 Kood bu Python bu tàmbali ngir porogaraam bi mën nañu ko am fi:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import os
import random
from pathlib import Path

def open_random_image(folder_paths, excluded_paths):
    # Převod cest na absolutní cesty
    folder_paths = [Path(folder).resolve() for folder in folder_paths]
    excluded_paths = [Path(excluded).resolve() for excluded in excluded_paths]

    # Seznam podporovaných přípon souborů
    supported_extensions = ['.png', '.jpg', '.jpeg', 'tif', 'tiff']

    # Seznam pro ukládání cest k obrázkům
    images = []

    # Prochází všechny zadané adresáře
    for folder_path in folder_paths:
        # Prochází soubory v adresáři a jeho podsložkách
        for path in folder_path.rglob('*'):
            # Přidává cesty k obrázkům, pokud mají podporovanou příponu
            if path.suffix.lower() in supported_extensions:
                images.append(path)

    while True:
        if not images:
            print("V zadaných adresářích nebyly nalezeny žádné obrázky.")
            return

        # Náhodný výběr souboru
        image_path = random.choice(images)

        # Kontroluje, zda vybraná cesta neleží v některém z vyloučených adresářů
        if any(image_path.is_relative_to(excluded) for excluded in excluded_paths):
            continue  # Pokud je cesta vyloučená, vybere jiný obrázek

        print(f"Otevírám obrázek: {image_path}")
        os.startfile(image_path)
        return

# Seznam cest k adresářům
folder_paths = [
    r"D:\\02_záloha dat",
    r"G:",
    r"K:"
    #r"C:"
]

# Seznam cest k vyloučení
excluded_paths = [
    r"K:\\WEB",
]

while True:  # Nekonečná smyčka
    open_random_image(folder_paths, excluded_paths)
    input("Stiskni ENTER pro opětovné spuštění nebo Ctrl+C pro ukončení programu...")

[/CODE BLOCK]

Fonksoŋ wàll
 
 samwiye 2024 
 
 
 
Prose bi mooy wone ni nu manee simulee tasaaroo ak tàggatug wàll ak ni ñuy àndee ak wàllu bi ñu nekk, ci ndimbalu modelu matematig ak simulaasioŋ bu ordinatëer. Jëfandikoo ay nattukaay yu amul yoon ngir dëkkal barab yi wàll yi di jóge, seen frekans ak seen amplitüd, ak dëkkal amplitüd bu wàññikoo bu ñuy soree barab bi, ak wàll yi di dellu ci wetu kërëg bi ak seeni tàggatug biir, di defar ay nataal yu neex xool yuy wone ni wàll yi di doxee ci barab bu yam.

Kood bu jóge
 Koodu Python bu jóge ngir prose bii ngi fi:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import os
import re

# Počet obrázků k vytvoření
num_images = 1

# Nastavení rozsahů pro náhodné hodnoty
frequency_range = (1, 50)  # Rozsah frekvencí vln
amplitude_range = (0.1, 1)  # Rozsah amplitud vln

# Počet zdrojů
number_of_sources = 3

# Velikost prostoru pro simulaci a rozlišení mřížky
space_size = 5
grid_resolution = 1000

for i in range(num_images):
    # Inicializace pole pro simulaci
    Z = np.zeros((grid_resolution, grid_resolution))

    # Vytvoření mřížky pro simulaci
    x = np.linspace(-space_size, space_size, grid_resolution)
    y = np.linspace(-space_size, space_size, grid_resolution)
    X, Y = np.meshgrid(x, y)

    # Generování náhodných zdrojů
    for _ in range(number_of_sources):
        source_position = np.random.uniform(-space_size, space_size, 2)
        frequency = np.random.uniform(*frequency_range)
        amplitude = np.random.uniform(*amplitude_range)

        # Výpočet vzdálenosti od zdroje a přidání vlny do simulace
        R = np.sqrt((X - source_position[0])**2 + (Y - source_position[1])**2)
        Z += amplitude * np.sin(frequency * R) / (R + 1)  # Přidání útlumu amplitudy

        # Odrážení vln od stěn
        for wall in [-space_size, space_size]:
            R_vertical = np.sqrt((X - 2*wall + source_position[0])**2 + (Y - source_position[1])**2)
            R_horizontal = np.sqrt((X - source_position[0])**2 + (Y - 2*wall + source_position[1])**2)
            Z += amplitude * np.sin(frequency * R_vertical) / (R_vertical + 1)
            Z += amplitude * np.sin(frequency * R_horizontal) / (R_horizontal + 1)

    # Vykreslení výsledku
    plt.figure(figsize=(10, 10))
    plt.imshow(Z, cmap='gray', extent=[-space_size, space_size, -space_size, space_size])
    plt.axis('off')
    
    # Před uložením obrázku použijte tight_layout pro optimalizaci rozložení
    #plt.tight_layout()

    # Uložení obrázku
    pattern = r"wave_interference_pattern_(\d+).png"
    existing_files = [f for f in os.listdir('.') if re.match(pattern, f)]
    if existing_files:
        numbers = [int(re.search(pattern, f).group(1)) for f in existing_files]
        max_number = max(numbers)
    else:
        max_number = 0
    file_name = f"wave_interference_pattern_{max_number + 1:05d}.png"
    plt.savefig(file_name, bbox_inches='tight', pad_inches=0, transparent=False, dpi=(grid_resolution*1.3)//10)
    plt.close()  # Zavření obrázku po uložení

[/CODE BLOCK]

Yoonu kurseer
 
 ut 2023 
 
 
 
Mën naa mel ne jëfante ak souris bu ordinatëer bi ay fan bu nekk, yëf la yu amul solo. Waaye lu muy doon su fekkone ne mën nañu soppi yëngu-yëngu yu mel ni yii ba ñu doon lu gën a rëy, lu gën a wone ni nit ki di liggéeyee ci ordinatëer bi? Ci ndimbalu kood bi di jël dëkkuwaayu kurseer bi ak melow pixel bi nekk ci suufam, xalaat bii mën na am.
 
Naka la muy liggéeyee?
 Kood bii di topp fu kurseer bi nekk ci ekran bi ak melow pixel bi nekk ci suufam. Xibaar yii dañu leen di yónnee ci saasi ci ab tableau bu virtual bu am rëy-rëyu ekran bi. Buleen nekk di yëngal souris bi, kood bi di nataal pixel bu am melow bi nekk ci suuf kurseer bi, ci dëkkuwaay bi kurseer bi nekk. Loolu day tekki ne bu ñuy liggéeyee ci ekran bi di yëngu, ci tableau bi lañuy sos ay yoon yu am melo. Yoon yii ay yoon yu amul moroom lañu te dañuy wone liggéey bi ak jëfante bi nit ki di def ak ordinatëer bi.
 
Lu muy xew bu yaggé?
 Su fekkone ne liggéey bii di dox lu yagg, tableau bi di na fees te yoon yi duñu gën a feeñ. Ci dëkk bi, tableau bi di na mel ni ab xibaar bu amul benn yoon (pseudonáhodný šum). Waaye am na yenn barab ci ekran bi yoo xam ne duñu soppi lu bari ci jamono. Mën na doon, ci misaal, barab bi nekk ci suufu Windows, fu ay ikoonu logiciel di nekk. Barab yii ci tableau bi dañuy des di mel ni lu nekk ci anam bu nekk.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-1.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-1.png] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-2.png]

[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-3.png]

[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-4.png]

[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-5.png]

Teel-teel
 Kood bii di wone jokkoo ci diggante teknoloji ak mbindaan, loolu di tax ñu mën a soppi jëfante bi ñuy def ak ordinatëer bi ba mu doon lu neex a xool. Doonte ci kanamu nit ki mën na mel ne liggéey ci ordinatëer bi ay yëf la yu amul solo, liggéey bii di wone ne sax yëngu-yëngu yii mën nañu am seeni rafetay ak seeni yoon.
 
Koodu xibaar
 Koodu Python bi ñu jëfandikoo ci liggéey bii mën nga ko fekk fii:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
 import pygame
import pyautogui
import sys
import time

# Inicializace Pygame
pygame.init()

# Získání rozměrů obrazovky
screen_info = pygame.display.Info()
screen_width = screen_info.current_w
screen_height = screen_info.current_h

# Vytvoření okna o rozměrech shodných s rozměry obrazovky
screen = pygame.display.set_mode((screen_width, screen_height))

# Hlavní smyčka programu
running = True
start_time = time.time()
while running:
    for event in pygame.event.get():
        if event.type == pygame.QUIT:
            running = False
        elif event.type == pygame.KEYDOWN:
            if event.key == pygame.K_ESCAPE:  # Když stisknete klávesu ESC, okno se zavře
                running = False

    # Získání souřadnic kurzoru pomocí PyAutoGUI
    x, y = pyautogui.position()

    # Získání barvy pixelu pod kurzorem
    if 0 <= x < screen_width and 0 <= y < screen_height:  # Zkontrolujte, zda jsou souřadnice v rámci obrazovky
        try:
            r, g, b = pyautogui.pixel(x, y)
            # Nabarvení pixelu na plátně touto barvou
            screen.set_at((x, y), (r, g, b))
        except:
            pass

    # Aktualizace zobrazení
    pygame.display.flip()

    # Uložení záložního obrázku každé 3 minuty
    if time.time() - start_time >= 180:  # 180 sekund = 3 minuty
        pygame.image.save(screen, "output_backup.png")
        start_time = time.time()

# Uložení obrázku
pygame.image.save(screen, "output.png")

# Ukončení Pygame
pygame.quit()
sys.exit()
[/CODE BLOCK]

TITLE: Doxu garab yi

============================================================
Doxu garab yi
 
 suwe 2023 
 
 
 
Tàmbalee
 Ci biir téere bi, dinañu seeti ab poroje bu boole àdduna garab yi, elektonik, porogaraam ak mbindu, ak mbindu-këyit yu neex. Poroje bi dafay jëfandikoo Arduino ak Python ngir sos ay wone yu neex yu jëlee ci dundin garab yi.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-1.png | arduino schéma zapojení]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-1.png] 
 
Nan lay liggéeyee?
 Arduino bi am na ñetti sànsër – bu natt tàngoor, bu natt leer, ak bu natt tooy-tooy. Sànsër yii dañu leen di teg ci biir potu garab yi (lu mel ni patison, poow, lëmbëy, bëy, kresson, piment, yeneen). Sànsër yi dañuy dëkk di dajale ay xibaar ci tàngoor, leer, ak tooy-tooy ci suuf si.
 Porogaraam bi dafay sos ab kànwas bu am 100×100 piksel. Piksel bu nekk ci kànwas bi dañu koy lëkkal ak xibaar yi ñu jële ci sànsër yi. RGB bu piksel bi dañu koy natt ci ni R (xonk) di tàngoor, G (vert) di leer, ak B (bul) di tooy-tooy.
 
Wone bu ab bëccëg
 Bu njëkk, dinañu seeti ab yoon bu yomb ci poroje bi, fu ab nataal di wone ab bëccëg ci dundin garab yi. Porogaraam bi dafay tàmbalee lëkkal piksel bu njëkk bi ci wetu cammoñ, ba ci kaw, te di lëkkal piksel bu nekk ci ni xibaar yi di ñëwee. Bu piksel bi lëkkalee, porogaraam bi dafay xaar ab waxtu bu néew ba pare di dem ci piksel bi toftoo. Bu agsee ci mujj gi ci lay dem ci tàmbale gi ci lay toftoo lëkkal. Liggéey bi di dem ba kànwas bi yépp lëkk.

Boole ay wone
 Bu wone bi ab bëccëg paree, mën nañu yokk liggéey bi ngir sos ay nataal yu bari yu wone ay waxtu yu gëna gudd.
 Mën nañu boole ay nataal yu nekk ci biir kàre, ba sos ay nataal yu gëna rëy. Ndaxte bu wone bi paree, leneen di tàmbalee, amul ab tàmbale bu tax waxtu bi di tàggoo. Ci anam yii, mën nañu sos ay nataal yu am 100x500 piksel, bu fekkee dañu leen boole ci juróomi wone.

Guddal waxtu bu diggante natt yi
 Waxtu bu diggante natt bu piksel bu nekk mën nañu ko guddal. Ci anam yii, ab kàre bu 100×100 piksel mën na wone du ab bëccëg rekk, waaye ay bëccëg yu bari. Njaqam yii dañuy may ñu topp dundin garab bi ci ay waxtu yu gudd ci ab nataal.
 
Doxu lëmbëy:
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-13.jpg]

  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-14.png] 


 
 
 
Boole ay njaqam
 Ci boole ñaari njaqam yii, mën nañu sos ay nataal yu bari, fu nataal bu nekk di wone ay bëccëg, te yépp di wone ay ayubés walla ay weer. Ci anam yii, mën nañu topp dundin garab bi ci gudd.
 
Doxu lëmbëy:
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-15.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-15.jpg] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-16.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-16.png] 
 
Doxu lëmbëy:
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-17.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-17.jpg] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-18.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-18.png] 
 
 
Firi ay njariñ
 Nataal yi ñu am dañuy may ñu gis dundin garab yi ci anam bu wute. “Lëkk” yu bul yi ci nataal bi dañuy wone guddi, ndaxte dafa doon tooy, waaye tàngoor bi du bari te leer bi du am. Ci wet ga, “lëkk” yu mboq yi dañuy am ndaxte ci mujj gi ci bëccëg bi, leeru jant bi dafa tàmbalee fekk garab bi, loolu tax tàngoor bi ak leer bi yokk. Loolu dafa feeñ ci nataal bi ni yokkug xonku ak vert, bi di sos mboq. Ci yeneen waxtu, mën nañu gis ay lëkk yu néew ci tàmbale guddi gi, ndaxte dafa amoon waxtu yu ma doon tàkk lamp bi ci biir kër gi. Ci yeneen wone, mën nañu gis ci leer bi bu wàññikoo ne, lu mel ni, dafa taw.
 
Kood bu tàmbalee
 Kood bu Python bi ngir poroje bi mën nañu ko am fi:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import serial
import pygame
import pygame.image
import decimal
import datetime

# Define constants
MAX_8BIT = 255
MAX_10BIT = 1023
WIDTH = 100
HEIGHT = WIDTH
SERIAL_PORT = 'COM3'
DELAY = 20 * 1000
MAX_RETRIES = 5
MIN_HUMIDITY = 750
MAX_HUMIDITY = 850

def actual_time():
    now_raw = str(datetime.datetime.now())
    now = now_raw.replace(":", "-")
    return now

def temperature_to_interval(temperature):
    return 1 / (1 + 2**(-1 * (temperature - 22)))

def remap_humidity(humidity, min_measured, max_measured):
    humidity_range = max_measured - min_measured
    humidity_proportion = (humidity - min_measured) / humidity_range
    lightness = int(humidity_proportion * MAX_10BIT)
    return min(max(lightness, 0), MAX_10BIT)

def max_255(v):
    return min(v, MAX_8BIT)

def remap_10bit_to_8bit(number):
    if not 0 <= number <= MAX_10BIT:
        raise ValueError("Input number must be between 0 and 1023")
    return round(number / 4)

def separate_data(data):
    temp, light, hum = map(float, data.split("|"))
    return temp, light, hum

def write_to_log(log):
    with open('log.txt', 'a') as f:
        f.write(log + "\n")

def read_arduino_data(ser, delay):
    data1 = ser.readline().decode().strip()
    pygame.time.wait(delay)
    data2 = ser.readline().decode().strip()
    pygame.time.wait(delay)
    data3 = ser.readline().decode().strip()
    pygame.time.wait(delay)
    if len(data1) != len(data2) or len(data2) != len(data3):
        return read_arduino_data(ser, delay)
    return data1, data2, data3

ser = serial.Serial(port=SERIAL_PORT, baudrate=115200)

pygame.init()
win = pygame.display.set_mode((WIDTH, HEIGHT))
surface = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, HEIGHT))
pygame.display.set_caption("TEMPERATURE + LIGHT + HUMIDITY")

for i in range(5):
    data_test = ser.readline().decode().strip()
    pygame.time.wait(100)

x = 0
y = 0
img_num = 0
sensitivity = 1
start_time = actual_time()
running = True

while running:
    for event in pygame.event.get():
        if event.type == pygame.QUIT:
            finish_time = actual_time()
            pygame.display.update()
            name = str(img_num)
            pygame.image.save(surface, '{}, START {}, FINISH {} (incomplete).png'.format(name, start_time, finish_time))
            print("IMAGE SAVED BUT INCOMPLETE")
            write_to_log("IMAGE SAVED BUT INCOMPLETE")
            running = False
    if running == False:
        break

    for _ in range(MAX_RETRIES):
        try:
            data1, data2, data3 = read_arduino_data(ser, DELAY)
            break
        except Exception:
            print("COULD NOT READ THE DATA, TRYING AGAIN...")
            write_to_log("COULD NOT READ THE DATA, TRYING AGAIN...")
            pygame.time.wait(500)
    else:
        print("FAILED TO READ DATA AFTER {} RETRIES".format(MAX_RETRIES))
        write_to_log("FAILED TO READ DATA AFTER {} RETRIES".format(MAX_RETRIES))
        continue

    Rraw = (separate_data(data1)[0] + separate_data(data2)[0] + separate_data(data3)[0]) / 3
    R = round(max_255(temperature_to_interval(Rraw) * MAX_8BIT * sensitivity))

    Graw = (separate_data(data1)[1] + separate_data(data2)[1] + separate_data(data3)[1]) / 3
    G = round(max_255(remap_10bit_to_8bit(Graw) * sensitivity))

    Braw = (separate_data(data1)[2] + separate_data(data2)[2] + separate_data(data1)[2]) / 3
    B = remap_humidity(Braw, min_measured=MIN_HUMIDITY, max_measured=MAX_HUMIDITY)
    B = round(max_255(remap_10bit_to_8bit(B) * sensitivity))

    color = [R, G, B]
    pygame.draw.rect(win, color, [x, y, 1, 1])
    surface.set_at((x, y), (color[0], color[1], color[2]))
    if (x + 1) < WIDTH:
        pygame.draw.rect(win, [0, 255, 0], [x + 1, y, 1, 1])
    pygame.display.flip()

    #   print current data with colored text
    if color[0] + color[1] + color[2] > 127:
        text_color = "0"
    else:
        text_color = "255"
    log = "COLOR: [{}, {}, {}],  TEMP.: {:.2f}°C, LIGHT: {}, HUM.: {},  TIME: {},  IMAGE NUMBER: {},   COMPLETED: {:.3f}%,  x: {}, y: {}" \
        .format(R, G, B, Rraw, round(Graw), round(Braw), actual_time(), img_num, (WIDTH * y + x + 1) / (WIDTH * HEIGHT) * 100, x, y)
    print('\033[38;2;{};{};{}m\033[48;2;{};{};{}m'
          .format(text_color, text_color, text_color, str(color[0]), str(color[1]), str(color[2])) +
          log +
          '\033[0m')
    write_to_log(log)

    x += 1
    if x >= WIDTH:
        x = 0
        y += 1
    if y >= HEIGHT:
        y = 0
        finish_time = actual_time()
        pygame.display.update()
        name = str(img_num)
        pygame.image.save(surface, '{}, START {}, FINISH {}.png'.format(name, start_time, finish_time))
        print("IMAGE SUCCESSFULLY SAVED")
        write_to_log("IMAGE SUCCESSFULLY SAVED")
        pygame.draw.rect(win, [0, 0, 0], [0, 0, WIDTH, HEIGHT])
        surface.fill((0, 0, 0))
        img_num += 1
        start_time = actual_time()
[/CODE BLOCK]
 
 
Mujju
 Poroje bi ab misaal bu rafet la ci ni ñu mënë jëfandikoo teknoloji ngir gëna xam dundin garab yi. Nataal yi ñu am dañuy may ñu gis dundin garab yi ak ni ñuy nangu ay xibaar.
 
Am na solo ñu xam ne, kooke poroje bi di may ab gis-gis bu wute ci dundin garab yi, du wone yépp ci ni ñuy dundee. Garab yi dañuy mën a nangu ay xibaar yu bari yu jóge ci seeni wet, lu mel ni tooy-tooy, ni suuf si di doxee, ak yeneen. Poroje bi du bëgg a wone dundin garab bi yépp, waaye dafay seeti ñetti xibaar yu am solo yu mën a natt.
 
Xamuñu nan la garab yi di firi xibaar yii, waaye xam nañu ne dañuy tontu. Poroje bi ab jéego la ngir tekki dundin yii ci anam bu ñu mën a gëna xam.

SOURCE: src/content/posts/rekurze-programu-ms-word.mdx
BUILT_HTML: rekurze-programu-ms-word/index.html
============================================================
Rekurse ci MS Word
 
 nowambar 2022 
 
 
 
Gis naa anam bi nuuy defee ngir tàmbalee proces bu rekurse ci Microsoft Word. Ñaari mbir a soxla – anam bu jokkoo ak tabloo. Ci anam bu jokkoo, mën nga dugal biir dokimaan A ci biir dokimaan B, ba bu biir bi soppee ci dokimaan A, soppee yooyu di feeñ ci dokimaan B. Ci yeneen kàddu – biir dokimaan B di tekki biir dokimaan A. Rekurse mën na tàmbalee ci sos biir bu tekki boppam. Naka lañu koy defee? Dafa doy rek nga sos dokimaan A (nu tudde ko ci misaal doc A.docx) ak dokimaan B (doc B.docx), ci dokimaan A sos tabloo bu amul dara, copy ko te dugal ko ci dokimaan B ci anam bu jokkoo. Tabloo bii nu yees dugal, dafa doy rek nga copy ko ci dokimaan B te dugal ko ci biir tabloo bi nekk ci dokimaan A ci anam bu jokkoo. Loolu di sos tabloo bu tàmbalee dugal boppam ci biir boppam. Ci lu ëpp, MS Word di tàmbalee tàq ba mujj daal di daanu. Mën nga gis anam bii yépp ci widiyo bi nekk ci suuf.
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/xaE-eYQw4Mw]

TITLE: Pseudos-poesie
SLUG: pseudobasen
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/pseudobasen/
LANGUAGE: wo
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2022-05-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/pseudobasen.mdx
BUILT_HTML: pseudobasen/index.html
============================================================
Pseudos-poesie
 
 me 2024 2022 
 
 
 
Lepp yëpp yi nekk ci poésii bii (ci li nu xam, te bàyyi baat yu gëstu yi) ay pseudos-baat lañu; loolu mooy ay baat yuy mel ni yu nekk ci làkk bu Czech te mën nañu am – ndaxte yëngu-yëngu yi ci biir baat yooyu mën nañu nekk ci làkk boobu – waaye amul tekki te duñu ko jëfandikoo. Poésii bi amul njariñ, te li ñuy tekki ci biir xelam dafa jublu ci li nuy xalaat, donte ay baat yu am solo ci wàllu kàddu yi ñu bind ci biir poésii bi mën nañu yëngal xel yi.
 
Lii di jéem du woon ngir sos ab téere bu am solo ci wàllu poésii, waaye ngir foowee rekk li làkk bi mën di def. Dafa neex xalaat ne, ci pseudos-baat bu nekk, mën nañu xam lu mu doon ci wàllu gramer ak li mu mën di tekki.
  
JEZÁPEK

V Městnale se krůcky škvoury
 K mochci ďáry vyněraly
 Pulstočmý kroch zanářel
 Do stupí honkaje vořenky
 Když jezápek kokeřil pod husečným vachalem koltěním zmán

Hlůmajíce zlanivou vostrčlici
 Sníkali garánci garánkám vrůzy
 A lačili jezápka zastrožlým přímanem
 Zastrožlým z blímného vesepství
 Dokřmeným lipačskou vrýnou

Hrů hrů! kafolčil jezápek zpufněle
 A holivěl likajky garánkám
 Až z hlemuňků kriskaly baťafky
 A cuchraly zrepčeným blizalkám
 Jalvičné zromky
  
Donte poésii bi amul njariñ, bu ñu koy jàng, mën na am lu xel bi di xalaat. Dañu bëggoon xam lan la xel bu ñu sos (AI) di xalaat ci loolu. Dëppoo nañu ay jumtukaay yu bari (ru-DALL-E, Dream, Deep Daze, starryai – Altair, starryai – Orion ak Night Café), yuy mën di def ay nataal bu ñu leen joxee kàddu. Jumtukaay bu nekk dafa wuute, yenn yi dañuy def ay nataal yu mel ni foto, yenn yi dañuy def ay nataal yu gëna nekk ci xalaat, te yenn yi dañuy def ay nataal yu gëna neex xool. Dañu wuute itam ci li ñuy xam làkk bu Czech (lu mel ni ru-DALL-E dafa xam làkk bu Czech bu baax).
 
Bàtt bu nekk ci poésii bi ñu jox ko jumtukaay yi, te jumtukaay bu nekk def na ay nataal yu wuute ci li mu xalaat ci bàtt boobu. Ci nataal yii, def nañu ab video bu ñuy woné li ñu xalaat, te ñuy jàng poésii bi ci biir. Jumtukaay bu Deep Daze mën na sax di filmaale li mu di def, ba tax ñu mën di gis ni nataal bi di gëna leeré.
 
ruDALL-E
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/CniEMECxcgw]
 
 
Dream
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/MIS05zpqcWA]
 
 
starryai (Altair)
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/qsmicN46yBk]

Generatoru limu yu amul yoon
 
 17. 2. 2022 - 27. 3. 2022, Karlovy Vary 
 
 Galeri bu mbindu Karlovy Vary: KARLOVARSKÝ SALON 22/2

[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/vystavy-generator-nahodnych-cisel-2-obr-8.jpg]

TITLE: Jaayndé bu gàtt ci dund gu nekk ci suuf
SLUG: strucna-historie-zivota-zemi
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/strucna-historie-zivota-zemi/
LANGUAGE: wo
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2021-03-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/strucna-historie-zivota-zemi.mdx
BUILT_HTML: strucna-historie-zivota-zemi/index.html
============================================================
Jaayndé bu gàtt ci dund gu nekk ci suuf
 
 mars 2021 
 
 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/1R8M4nRLPWQ]
 
 
Video bi mi ngi juddoo ci liggéeyu jàngu ci Střední odborné škole obchodu, užitého umění a designu bu Plzeň (Nerudova 33).
 
Benn nataal ci video bii di teewal 200 000 at ci jaayndé suuf si. Loolu di mat na jamono ji nitu Homo sapiens dundee. Kon itam, nit ki ñu ko nataal ci biir nataal bu mujj bi ci video bi.
 
Yépp nataal yi ñu jëfandikoo ci video bi, ki ko def mooy ki leen nataal.

TITLE: Dund bi Hromnické jezírko
SLUG: zivot-hromnickem-jezirku
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku/
LANGUAGE: wo
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2021-01-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku.mdx
BUILT_HTML: zivot-hromnickem-jezirku/index.html
============================================================
Dund bi Hromnické jezírko
 
 samwé 2021 
 
 
 

[MediaObject: https://www.google.com/maps/embed?pb=!1m18!1m12!1m3!1d2873.387394621076!2d13.444731496763541!3d49.85061064085366!2m3!1f0!2f0!3f0!3m2!1i1024!2i768!4f13.1!3m3!1m2!1s0x0%3A0x93e249730f9d76f5!2zSHJvbW5pY2vDqSBqZXrDrXJrbw!5e1!3m2!1scs!2scz!4v1609929799218!5m2!1scs!2scz]

 
Hromnické jezírko [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hromnick%C3%A9_jez%C3%ADrko] ab na dëkk bu nekk ci diiwaan bu Plzeň-sever. Ndaxte yoonu bëtt bëtt bi ñu doon def ci jamono yu weesu, ndoxum lëkk bi dafa nekk ay ndox yu am ay asid yu bari (am na pH bu tollu ci 2.6 – 2.9). Loolu moo tax ci ay xibaar yu bari ñu bind ne «ndox mi amul dund te amul yaram». Ba ci takk-der bu ñu teg ci wetu yoon wi [https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/d/d0/Tabule_Hromnick%C3%A9_jez%C3%ADrko.JPG] ñu bind nañu: «… lu dul ay garab yu ndaw, [ndox mi] amul dara lu fay dund». Loolu gëmuluma ko woon ndaxte dund dafa am doole ci toppatoo. Kon nag, dogal naa dem seeti ko te xool ko sama bopp.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-1.jpg | Hromnické jezírko]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-1.jpg] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-2.jpg | natt pH]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-2.jpg] 
 
Ci 19. desambar 2020 laa jël ay ndox ci Hromnické jezírko. Dund gu am ay yaram yu bari feeñ na ci biir ndox mi bi lëkk bi wàccé. Gis naa ci ay dënn yu pakomár.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-3.jpg | dënn bu pakomár]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-3.jpg] 
 
Gis naa itam dënn bu mbooc, bi ma mënë xam ci ndimbalu mikroskop.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-4.jpg | dënn bu mbooc]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-4.jpg] 
 
Gis naa itam ay yaram yu am benn yaram, ni ay krásnoočko, yu bari woon lool ba ñu nekk ay xob yu vert ci kaw ndox mi te mën nañu leen gis ak sunu bët.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-5.jpg | krásnoočka]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-5.jpg] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-6.jpg | krásnoočka]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-6.jpg] 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/aoPDRJ_XaxI]
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-7.jpg | krásnoočko]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-7.jpg] 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/XbHSxjzpSYE]
 
 
Ci nataal yi ci kaw, mën nañu gis ay yaram yu prokaryotic – ay sinice (ay bakterie yu ndaw yu mel ni ay lëkk). Beneen yaram bu prokaryotic bi ma gis ci ndox mi, mooy bakterie bi (bi ma mënul woon xam bu baax ba léegi).
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-8.jpg | bakterie]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-8.jpg] 
 
Bi ma ko xoolé ñaari ay weer, gis naa sax ay nálevníky. Bi ci njëkk mooy vířenka, bi ñaareel bi mooy uroleptus.
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-9.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-9.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-10.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-10.jpg] 
 
 
Dafa neex xalaat ni yaram yu bari mën nañu dund ci biir ndox mu am asid bu bari ni loolu. Loolu mooy firndeel ne wax ji ne ndox mi amul dund, dëggul. Kon war nañu soppi takk-der bi ak ay site internet yu turism yu ko bindoon. Waaye loolu du yaram yépp yi nekk ci Hromnické jezírko, kon dina neex ñu def yeneen natt ci yeneen jamono, walla ñu jël ndox ci lu yagg (ci suuf, ndax amul oksijen ak leer, mën na am ay bakterie yu dund ci anam yu dul soxla oksijen).
