TITLE: Njëlu melo
SLUG: barevny-prumer
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/barevny-prumer/
LANGUAGE: wo
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2017-04-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/barevny-prumer.mdx
BUILT_HTML: barevny-prumer/index.html
============================================================
Njëlu melo
 
 awril 2017 
 
 
 
Ci wetu cammoon bi ci nataal bi, am na 1156 ay nataal yu nekk ci 34x34 px. Pixel bu nekk ci melo dafay teewal melo bu nekk ci diggu nataal bu nekk ci réew moomu. Ci wetu ndeyjoor bi ci nataal bi, am na melo bu nekk ci diggu yépp pixel yi jóge ci wetu cammoon bi, maanaam melo bu nekk ci diggu yépp nataal yi jóge ci réew moomu.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barevny-prumer-obr-1.png | Réewum Cek]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barevny-prumer-obr-1.png]

Tank
 
 awril 2017

  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/tank-obr-1.jpg]

  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/tank-obr-2.jpg] 

 
[MediaObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/files/tank-dokument.pdf]
 
 
 
Ci weeru desambar 2012 laa nataaloon ab tank bu xare ci réewum Israyil, ci gàncaxu Negev. Ci kood ANSI bu nataay bi, soppi naa lépp lu doon biddiw (*) ba nekk kërëss (†). Nataay bi ma soppi noonu laa ko dellu dëgërël.

Morso suukër
 
 mars 2017 
 
 
 
Série nataal yi juddoo na ci jamono ju ñu doon def ay nattukaay ngir duggu ci Sekkool bu mag bu njàngale njàngum njariñ, mbindum mbind ak nattukaay ci Plzeň (Nerudova 33).
 
Ngir xaaj bi ci nataal bi, mën nañu woon indi sunu ay jumtukaay, waaye amoon na dogal ci lu jëm ci lim bi ak tolluwaayu yëf yi. Indiwon naa ab boyit bu morso suukër, di leen doxal ci kaw ab sàttala ngir ñu nekk ci ab ràndu bu jub, ba noppi laa leen sànni ndox. Morso yi di yàqoo, di rot, te di daanu ci anam bu neex.

TITLE: Bibliyoteeku street art
SLUG: street-art-knihovna
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/street-art-knihovna/
LANGUAGE: wo
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2017-03-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/street-art-knihovna.mdx
BUILT_HTML: street-art-knihovna/index.html
============================================================
Bibliyoteeku street art
 
 mars 2017 
 
 
 
Dëkk bi fëpp la nu nekk, am na ay liggéey yu am solo yu street art, yu jóge ci ay stickers yu ndaw, ay mur yu ñu nataal, ba ci ay xeeti yaram yu 3D. Ndaxte ñi koy def dañuy wara nekk ci biir gëstu ngir bañ ñu leen di jàpp, àddunuy street art bi amul solo ci wàllu komers. Street art yi nu gis fëpp ci dëkk bi, dañuy gëna yees. Street art yu yees yi dañuy wëlbati yu yàgg yi, lëkk bi di rot ak mur bi, yaram yu 3D yi dañuy yàq, te stickers yi dañuy rot walla ñu wëñ ci biir jamono. Loolu ab yoon la bu nekk ci dund, ab wàllu jamono, waaye gëm naa ne am na yenn street art yi war nañu gëna dund.
 
Te loolu moo tax ma tànn liggéey yi gëna am solo ngir bind leen. Ngir loolu, ci atum 2017 laa sos ab xët ci Facebook bu tudd Bibliyoteeku street art, ba léegi ma ngi fay def ay yëg-yëg. Ngir àgg ci nit ñi gëna bari, sos naa itam ab xët ci Instagram bu amoon tur boobu.
 
Ci xët yii, ma ngi gëna fexe ba duggal liggéey yi am ay xalaat, te ma ngi gis ne mbirum gëstu bi ci wàllu nataal bi, amul solo lool.
 
Bibliyoteeku street art (Facebook):
 www.facebook.com/streetartknihovna [https://www.facebook.com/streetartknihovna]
 
Bibliyoteeku street art (Instagram):
 www.instagram.com/streetartknihovna [https://www.instagram.com/streetartknihovna]
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/street-art-knihovna-obr-1.jpg | Screenshot bu xëtu Facebook]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/street-art-knihovna-obr-1.jpg] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/street-art-knihovna-obr-2.jpg | Screenshot bu xëtu Instagram]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/street-art-knihovna-obr-2.jpg]

Du amul solo fu nga jóge...
 
 11. 7. 2016 - 14. 8. 2016, Plzeň 
 
 DJKT (Nová scéna): Plzeňský salón 2016 
 
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2-obr-1.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2-obr-1.jpg]

[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2-obr-3.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2-obr-3.jpg] 
 
 
 
 
28. ci weeru desambar 2014 laa nataal ab bëtt bu yàgg bu am ay bind ci Mombasa. Gannaaw loolu, ma ngi doon gëstu ngir xam li ci bind. Bind bi ma mën a jàng ci biir loolu laa dugal ci biir internet, te loolu moo tax ma gis nataal bii di topp.
 

 

It doesn't matter which religion you claim you're. It doesn't matter which country you're coming from. It doesn't matter whether you 're poor or rich. It doesn't matter if you're black or white. We are all the same in the eyes of God.

 

 

Nezáleží, jakého jsi vyznání. Nezáleží, z jaké země pocházíš. Nezáleží, jestli jsi chudý nebo bohatý. Nezáleží, jestli jsi černoch nebo běloch. Všichni jsme si rovni v očích Boha.

 https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=jxg-6iVPPmk

TITLE: Ba ci dëkk bi ba ci mbëkk
SLUG: od-mesta-az-po-nahodu
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/od-mesta-az-po-nahodu/
LANGUAGE: wo
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2016-01-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/od-mesta-az-po-nahodu.mdx
BUILT_HTML: od-mesta-az-po-nahodu/index.html
============================================================
Ba ci dëkk bi ba ci mbëkk
 
 samwiye 2016 
 
 
 
Ci atum 2016 laa jotoon liggéey bu ma waroon def ngir sàkku dugg ci ekoolu mbindaan ak nataal bu nekk ZUŠ Plzeň, Jagellonská 14, di stylisation bu dëkk bi. Dama doon laaj nan la stylisation bi mënë tollu, te loolu tax na ma liggéey ci loolu ba léegi (atum 2019). Jóge naa ci stylisation bu yomb bu dëkk bi ba ci yëngal rastr yu yoon, loolu di indi ab yëngu bu am solo ci wàllu nataal. Gannaaw loolu, ma ngi jéemoon na jëfandikoo mbëkk ci liggéey bi, waaye mësuma gis dara lu mel ni mbëkk bu dëgg-dëgg te amul sabab. Determinism mooy doon teree, loolu tax ma doon jëfandikoo pseudonahoda (mën nga xam sabab bi, waaye njariñ bi dafa yombul a xam, loolu tax mu mel ni mbëkk). Yenn yoon yi dañu ma doon yóbbu ci yoon yu tëj, loolu tax ma wut yeneen yoon ak yeneen xeeti grafika, ni mel ni programování bu nataal, moiré effect, Fibonacci sequence, etc. Série nataal yi ci suuf mooy dëppoo liggéey yii.

  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/od-mesta-az-po-nahodu-obr-1.jpg]

============================================================
Du amul solo fu nga jóge...
 
 10. 10. 2015 - 11. 10. 2015, Plzeň

TITLE: Du amul solo fu nga jóge...
SLUG: nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel

LANGUAGE: wo
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2015-10-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel.mdx
BUILT_HTML: nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel/index.html
============================================================
Du amul solo fu nga jóge...
 
 oktoobar 2015 
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-obr-1.jpg | Nataalu Vojta Maur bi ñu def ci Mombasa, 2014]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-obr-1.jpg] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-obr-2.jpg | 28. desàmbar 2014 laa nataal ab mur bu yàgg bu am ay bind ci Mombasa. Gannaaw loolu, ma ngi doon gëstu ngir xam li ci bind. Bind bi ma mën a jàng ci biir loolu laa dugal ci internet bi, te loolu laa mën a fekk nataal bii topp.]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-obr-2.jpg] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-obr-3.jpg | Nataal bi ñu fekk ci internet bi]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-obr-3.jpg] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-obr-4.jpg | Du amul solo lu nga gëm. Du amul solo réewum fu nga jóge. Du amul solo ndax da nga nekk nit ku néew doole walla ku am alal. Du amul solo ndax da nga nekk nit ku ñuul walla ku weex. Ñun ñépp ay yam lañu ci kanamu Yàlla.]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-obr-4.jpg]

TITLE: Ñax
SLUG: plevel
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/plevel/
LANGUAGE: wo
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2008-05-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/plevel.mdx
BUILT_HTML: plevel/index.html
============================================================
Ñax
 
 me 2008 
 
 
 
Ligéey bu jóge ci daara.
 
Ndigël bi dafa doon ñu dëppi ay fóoy yu bari xeet ci kàggu, te def leen ay nataal.
 
Ci gannaaw fóoy yi, dama def plevel.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/plevel-obr-1.jpg | plevel]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/plevel-obr-1.jpg]
